Dear lawmakers, constitutional courts are institutions that observe the constitutions where the fundamental principles of the law are collected and have the final say on their conflicts. Although their names are different, institutions that have this function are found in many of today's governments. He entered the system in our country with the 1961 Constitution, and thus far he has continued with some legal provisions. As we know, the Constitutional Court has three basic tasks, such as auditing the constitution of laws, auditing political parties and the Supreme Court. Dear MPs, we believe that security, peace and economic growth can be achieved by establishing justice, as members of a civilization that put justice in the center of the state, saying that justice is the foundation of justice. In this sense, there have been serious developments in democratisation in Turkey, especially in the last decade, along with the EC PARTY governments, which came to power with our nation's democratic preferences. Serious reforms and structural changes have been implemented on the basis of basic rights and freedoms and the legal state. One of them is the right to an individual application with a 2010 constitutional amendment. The individual application is an extraordinary form of law applied to by individuals who have violated basic rights and freedoms by the public force. Its main objective is to determine the situations that cause rights violations and ensure that it is cleared by public authorities, preventing new violations from being exposed. The individual application has brought significant innovations to the Turkish legal system, and the Constitutional Court is no more than an institution that oversees the constitutional compliance of norms. The violations that our citizens face have become a judicial body that directly deals with the consumption of legal means. The total number of individual applications from September 23, 2012 to September 30th of this year was 201,547. The Constitutional Court has decided on about 80 per cent of these applications. The total number of files is 161,084. 79 per cent of these files are unacceptable decisions, 7 per cent are administrative rejection decisions, 12 per cent have been agreed to merge, and 2 per cent of the application has been decided to violate the rights. This suggests that the violation of rights is extremely low. In addition to implementing individual application, the Ministry of Justice has established the chairmanship of the Human Rights Compensation Commission. As of 2013, 8.865 applications have been submitted to this Commission. The decision of these applications has been made. The European Court of Human Rights adopted this Commission as an internal legal road and found many applications unacceptable on the grounds that it could be applied to the Human Rights Compensation Commission. These regulations have reduced applications for the European Court of Human Rights, and our country has gained credit for international law enforcement. Following the individual application, the Constitutional Court's number of members has been increased and the Grand National Assembly of Turkey has been authorized to elect members of the Constitutional Court. With these thoughts, I wish the Constitutional Court and all of our institutions' budgets to be good for our country, and I salute the High Council.

Article 114 of the 1961 Constitution, Article 125 of the 1982 Constitution, stipulated that all actions and procedures of the administration should be subject to judicial supervision. It is an effective and important judicial organisation in the judicial audit of the administration, as well as being an advisory and decision-making body that assists the executive body according to Article 155 of the Constitution. In this sense, it is a key guarantee of the principle of separation of forces and of course democracy. In this way, administrative transactions and decisions are intended to be established on the basis of state seriousness and justice, free of pleasure and periodicity. tbmm-2018-12-11sit01spe0068par0496	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of the AK PARTY Group on the offer of the Central Management Budget Law in the year 2019. I salute the High Council and our beloved nation. The budget is laws authorising the executive body to be granted permission and authority to collect public revenues and to make expenses available by the legislature. Turkey's Grand National Assembly uses its budgetary rights, while the Court oversees the results of its authority to collect public revenues on behalf of citizens and to spend those revenues on the executive body. In this context, the role of the Court, which has a long history and experience of 1506 years, is huge in the effective execution of the control activities of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey. The Court plans and conducts its audits on behalf of the Supreme Assembly in an honest, unbiased, neutral and in accordance with international control standards. The Court aims to contribute to public administration's transparency and accountability; it uses IT technology to a maximum extent in its controls; it constantly develops control and reporting capacity; it also takes into account the expectations and needs of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey, the public and audited public administrations. Mr. President, valuable lawmakers have also been signed by the EC PARTI governments to ensure that public financial management and control can be carried out in a way that is unconcerned, honest, transparent and in accordance with international control standards. In 2004, the amendment to Article 160 of the Constitution opened the way for military property to be audited by the Court without any restrictions. The 2005 Constitutional amendment developed the possibility that the audit of local governments could be made directly by the Court. Public Financial Management and Control Law 5018 has been adopted. Law 6216, which ensures that financial control of political parties is carried out by the Court, has been enforced. The Prime Minister's Supreme Control Board has handed it over to the Court, and the audit is held under one roof. The scope of control of public resources is also expanded by the Court. Law 832 has been made available only to make proper inspections, as well as performance and financial audits by Law No. 6085 as well as the Court's competency audit. Again, the reporting function with Law 6085 has been strengthened and required to explain it to the public. In line with all these reforms, an accountable, world-class control process has begun. The public spending has been efficient and the waste has been prevented, the acceleration of development and the implementation of sustainable development has also been opened up. There's a sense of trust between the state and the citizen. A transparent budget and transparent income, and the cost control mechanism is also built. Precious lawmakers have been audited by 99 per cent of the central management budget, 100 per cent of the social security institutions budget, and 73 per cent of the local administration budget under the control programme of 2017. Now, there's a Turkey that makes big budgets for our nation. There is a control mechanism that predicts income, costs, is transparent and manageable, with a transparency that can be achieved by the public at any moment. The Supreme Court's presidency, whose background, savings and services are an important part of our state tradition, is also in tight co-operation with international regional organisations of higher control institutions. Mr. President, the valued MPs used 217 million of the Court's total grant of 257 million in their final account in 2017. The Court's total budget law proposal for 2019 is $367m in order to achieve its goals and goals in the Strategic Plan and Performance Programme.

tbmm-2018-12-11sit01spe0071par0512	Mr. President, my esteemed congressman friends, as I begin to address the Council of Judges and Prosecutors' budget of 2019, one of the most important institutions of the justice organization that I was once proud to be a member of, I salute the High Council and our beloved nation. After the constitutional amendment, the Council of Judges and Prosecutors' High Council of Judges and Prosecutors has been renamed as the Council of Judges and Prosecutors, while the structure of the board and the electoral system have been reorganised. The number of members of the Council of Judges and Prosecutors after the constitutional amendment has been reduced from 22 to 13, from 3 to 2. The chairman of the board, the Minister of Justice, and the deputy minister of the Ministry of Justice, have been made a member of the board. Of the four members appointed by the presidency, three of them are from high judges, from the judiciary, and one member from one of our members is from the Supreme Court. The most beautiful privilege given to the Council of Judges and Prosecutors by the Constitutional Amendment here is that seven members have been chosen specially by our Parliament for a matter that is truly important to the nation. Of the three members of the Supreme Court, one member of the Court is a member of the Court; three members are among the teachers and the lawyers elected to serve in the legal branches of higher education institutions mentioned in the law; at least one of them is a professor and at least one of them is a lawyer. The Grand National Assembly of Turkey is voting in secret for each member of the candidates appointed by the commission. The first vote calls for two-thirds of the total number of members, three-thirds of the members in the second vote, if the required majority cannot be reached. If the second vote does not qualify, the membership election will be completed with a name-pulling procedure among the two candidates who received the most votes. Memberships are selected for four years, members whose term expires can be re-elected. tbmm-2018-12-11sit01spe0075par0538	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of the AK PARTY Group regarding the budget of the National Security Council Secretary General's office in 2019. On this occasion, I respectfully salute your high council. Each state must take precautions based on its own security perceptions of threats and risks directed at its existence, its strength, its integrity, and its prosperity. In line with this requirement, states are establishing national security systems that contain processes and elements that will enable monitoring and identifying threats and opportunities in the environment and around the world to determine Matuf politics and implement the right policies. In this framework, many states have created special structures under different names, such as the national security board or the defence board, in order to make healthy, holistic, fast decisions concerning national security and to enable sharing of information between ministries or institutions, consultation and coordination. The first step in the national security structure of our country was taken in 1933 by the High Defense Assembly, which was set up as a national mobilisation. Over the years, it has passed through a parallel shift to the development of security understanding, and has finally formed the National Security Council with the Constitution of 1982. The President's Vice President, Vice President of the National Security Council, who has a long history of eighty-five years and is one of the key components of our country's national security system, is provided by the meeting of Justice, National Defence, Interior and Foreign Affairs and Chiefs of Staff, Kara, Deniz, Air Force Commanders on a constitutional platform. The National Security Council, set by our President and gathered every two months, can also meet directly with the President's call if necessary. The National Security Council is the state's highest co-ordination board in the field of national security, and board meetings are a ground where national security issues are taken at the highest level by mutual exchange of ideas. The Secretary of the National Security Board is an institution with a long history, such as the National Security Council. It was organized under different names in the historical process, and is finally named after the Secretary General of the National Security Board. The presidential government system, which began on July 9, 2018, has a re-organisation of July 15, 2018 and presidential decree No.6. Under the presidential decree, the General Secretary-General has been assigned to carry out business and procedures related to the National Security Council meetings, to carry out the tasks provided by the board, and to do research and evaluations on these tasks, to get these research and evaluation results to the board members and to establish a documentary centre covering these issues and to conduct the work on the preparation of the National Security Policy Document. The head of the National Security Board's Secretary General is the Secretary General of the National Security Board. Directly appointed by our President, the Secretary General is tasked with carrying out the duties of the Secretary General in accordance with the laws and regulations of the President and the state's national security policy. The Secretary General is responsible to the President. Currently, 236 members of the National Security Council's General Secretary-General are staffed by different disciplines, with a degree in master's degree, mostly graduated from universities in international relations, economics, law. In view of the financial budget of the National Security Council for 2019, it is seen that 70 per cent are earmarked for personnel costs, 11 per cent for state premiums to social security institutions, 9 per cent for property and service costs, and 8 per cent for capital expenses. The budget budget, envisioned in the budget of 2019, rose by 10.92 per cent, according to the budget of 2018. The Secretary-General of the National Security Council continues with regular contacts with institutions that are responsible for its activities on financial budget drafting in 2019, and also attends internationally diverse meetings.

tbmm-2018-12-11sit02spe0297par1676	Mr. President, I would like to present our views on the budgets of the President and the Grand National Assembly of Turkey's Movement Party Group. On this occasion, I salute your high council and our beloved nation, watching over us on their screens. Today, I pray to our martyr, Rize County Police Commissioner, God willing, to have mercy on our wounded brothers and sisters. Dear lawmakers voted and approved the presidential government system with a public vote of the Turkish nation's constitutional amendment dated 16 April 2017, and elected the first President of the new system with the June 24th 2018 elections. A third term in our republic's history now began, along with the presidential government system, which took effect on July 9, 2018, when the president was sworn in. With the presidential government system, our country has been restored to a system of governance, where stability in governance is provided for justice in representation. The new government system has established strong governance, the requirements for the active Parliament, and has laid the groundwork for Turkey's ability to respond more strongly to threats. The most important thing about the new system is that it stops running from being two-headed. The possible state crisis with the Prime Minister and the Council of Ministers, which is on the other side of the administration because of the public-elected President's political responsibility towards the public and its broad executive powers, has been eliminated. Disputes of illegality at the top of the state have been terminated. Therefore, executive authority is granted to the President as a whole. In addition, the presidential and parliamentary elections are held on the same day every five years. So the government debates, the problem of government failure, the government crisis, the coalition period, it's over. The path to political, economic and democratic stability has been opened. A new Turkish system has been established that facilitates the solution to Turkey's problems, ensures the quick and effective decision-making of our state and the peace and prosperity of our nation. Instead of a unlimited official and irresponsible president, a presidential office has been established, which is supervised by both the judiciary and the law, as well as the nation's accountability. The president has been charged with criminal responsibility. The criminalization mechanism for this purpose has been replaced as a powerful control tool for the new system. All actions and procedures of the President have become subject to legislation. Vice presidents and ministers have also been subjected to the same procedure in all their crimes related to their duties. In addition, all actions and procedures of the President who had not previously been subject to judicial supervision have been subject to judicial supervision. In both the April 16th public vote and the June 24th elections, false and unsubstantiated allegations were raised to discredit and defame the presidency's government system, and all kinds of false news and gossip have been put into circulation, but our nation has not respected them or valued them. Now, why is it that every arrangement is called the President? He is the President of a political party; they say, "Is that okay?" Earlier, the Council of Ministers was called the President, the Prime Minister. Aren't the prime ministers a party leader, ministers a party member? Why is it that when the Council of Ministers is called the Prime Minister, it happens when the president is called the official, it doesn't happen? (AC PARTY) Clearly, those who are in captivity of their prejudices are far from grasping the facts. Those who talk about democracy must first stop resisting the will of the nation. The Constitutional amendment has been authorized to issue a decree, with the President's unlimited use clear and limited solely to the issues related to the administration. Besides, the President's decrees are not in law enforcement. The president is not authorized to issue a decree on basic rights, rights of persons and homework and political rights and homework. The President is unable to issue a decision on issues envisioned and on matters clearly regulated in the Constitution. However, the Council of Ministers had previously been able to issue a law decree on all matters under the law code; this practice was lifted, limiting the authority given to the administration. The presidential government system, the most important management reform in our republic's history, is the most dynamic democratic choice that responds to conditions and developments. The presidential government system is a model of governance in accordance with the state tradition of the Turkish nation's historical mission. The basic attributes of the republic, the first four articles of the Constitution, such as our national and unitary state structure, Turkish national identity, democratic regime and basic human rights, are also indispensable to the new system. The radicalization of the presidential government system and its health and perpetuation of all institutions and rules is what we are sensitively concerned about as the National Movement Party, and we find it important. In addition to the new system, some ministries have been merged into an effective co-ordination and the number of ministries has been reduced from 26 to 16, and has been restructured. In addition, the bureaucratic hierarchy has been modified, the deputy undersecretary and undersecretary have been removed, and the deputy ministers have been replaced. There are nine policy boards, four offices, one Executive Affairs Chairman and 11 other organisations under the framework of the regulations. According to the positions of policy boards, law, economy, science, technology and innovation, education and education, health and food, culture and art, local governance, social policies, security and foreign policy proposals have been envisioned, strategic and long-term visions. With the Investment Office, it was intended to open the way for investors; with the Financial Office; to develop new financial instruments; to become Turkey's center of attraction in finance; to provide efficient and efficient use of human resources; and to improve the public's performance. The digitalisation of the state with the Office of Digital Transformation, reducing documents, reducing management's burden with large data analysis and artificial intelligence practices, reducing power in Turkey's cyber security field, reducing bureaucracy, accelerating operations and increasing the quality of service. In this scope, we find the presidential organization structure and administrative structure important and valuable as the National Movement Party. Dear lawmakers have been established as President of the Republic in an effort to enlighten public opinion in time and accurate information, to facilitate the work of press members and to facilitate the development of the press, to co-ordinate communication strategies with relevant institutions to promote our country. The media, one of the essential elements of democratic society, is one of the most effective tools for society to learn the truth, to communicate healthyly with the environment and the world it lives in. As it will be remembered, the Turkish media has taken a firm stand against the July 15 coup attempt against the nation and democracy. Our media has contributed crucially to preventing a vicious coup attempt by continuing its broadcasts despite the intervention of the coupgers. As of today, our media is facing serious problems, and under difficult circumstances, it is committed with great sacrifice. Due to lack of income resources and the continued increase of costs, economic distress has been experienced. The satellite frequency fees set by TurkishSAT have increased greatly due to an increase in exchange rate. Televisions, especially local channels, have become unable to pay their fees. The entire imported paper and paper products industry has also been very negatively affected by the increase in currency, and the high rise in prices has hit publishing. Newspapers can't buy paper. Radios, televisions and newspapers are struggling to survive the high levels of increase in input costs. Some are closed and others are in danger of closing. Press employees are out of work, many journalists and reporters are laid off. Resolving our media's problems is of great importance to our democracy. A comprehensive change in the media field is needed to be renewed. A package for our media must be prepared immediately. The right expectations and demands of the media must be met. The increase in the publication fee of the official ads issued to the newspapers by the President's Decision 453, published in the Official Gazette today is satisfactory. In the meantime, the application for the request for a document on the fact that there is no overdue tax debt in the payment of official ads must be postponed. The President's calls for the use of Turkish lira are not noted that a public institution, TÜRKSAT (TÜRKSAT), has continued to apply satellite rent via currency. The price tariff imposed by TKSAT (TÜRKSAT) must be taken to a reasonable level and must be determined on Turkish lira. National, regional and local televisions and radios should be removed from the RTÜK share, an important expense pen. Tax cuts and conveniences for paper and paper products must be brought in. More importantly, measures for the paper industry must be taken immediately, and the paper we need must be produced in Turkey. The share of the local media's advertising cake is very limited. At least 15 per cent of public advertising must be made available to local media. Official programmes abroad must also be attended by representatives of the Anadolu media. Special support programmes must be put into practice so that the Anadolu media can continue its activities as a serious, media-based, neutral and objective publication. For this purpose, newspapers that can't keep up with developing technology should be put into place, such as in some sectors, for radio and televisions to modernise their printing facilities, for which they can renew their technology, without interest or affordable loan support for the Anadolu media. The Internet media, which has made great progress in recent years, must be legalised in publishing. The press workers perform their duties despite all negative conditions in order to deliver accurate and impartial information to the public. One of the main problems of press employees is negative working conditions. Press employees receive low wages and serve under difficult conditions during long hours. Only those with a yellow press card can benefit from the right of weariness. Press employees' rights and working conditions must be improved, and all of them should be able to benefit from the rights to wear out. Dear lawmakers, in this part of my speech, I will express our views on the budget of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey. The Constitutional amendment, adopted by a public vote on April 16, 2017, aims to bring the Grand National Assembly of Turkey to a more efficient and powerful structure. In this framework, the Grand National Assembly of Turkey has been restored to its original function and qualifications and has been strengthened in terms of representation and authority. In the making of the law, Parliament's will has been highlighted. Parliament is making law on law proposals made by lawmakers. The administration can only offer budget and final account law proposals, with the authority of the Parliament to approve the budget law proposal in full or to be changed. As a matter of fact, we're negotiating the budget as Congress, so it's obvious that the claims that the budget rights have been transferred are unrealistic. By the law of authority, the application to issue a decree under the rule of law has been lifted, which means the removal of the legislature's power of law. The state-of-the-art decision, which will be declared by the president, and the extension of the deadline, must be submitted to parliament's approval, so it is not true that the President has been given OPEC's authority, as it used to be, that Parliament must be approved. Moreover, it has been arranged that the presidential decrees, which will be issued in extraordinary circumstances, will be submitted to Parliament's approval on the day they are published in the Official Gazette, and if the decision is not reached within three months, it will automatically be lifted. The control functions of parliament, through question, general discussion, parliamentary research and parliamentary investigation have been strengthened. The President has been made accountable to the Parliament. The written question motions will be answered within 15 days. Instead of the Parliament, the people have been given direct authority. The administration no longer comes out of parliament and takes the vote of confidence from the nation itself, so the vote of confidence and the geneor mechanism has been lifted. The legal infrastructure for political parties to be able to participate in the election by making an alliance has been established, and the principle of justice has been observed in the representation. Given the increase in Turkey's population, the number of MPs has been increased to 600, the age of selection has been reduced to 18, and the representation of our young in Parliament has been opened, thus increasing the representation and coverage of parliament. Along with the new system, the administrative capacity of parliament must be developed in line with its capacity to make laws, to increase the capacity to propose laws. There is a need for additional capacity for lawmakers to provide a healthy analysis of their proposals. There is also a need to determine the procedures and principles related to the giving and meeting of law proposals. It is also important that regulations needed to be carried out should be transferred to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey and turned into a law proposal. Turkey's Grand National Assembly is organised in accordance with its working procedures and basics in the Civil Code. Many changes have been made in various substances over 45 years of the Turkish Grand National Assembly's Institution, which was put into effect in 1973. These changes have broken the Civil Code's system. It is clear that the Council of Parliament must be addressed with a new understanding, and that parliamentary work requires both institutional and functional innovation. In this framework, it is possible that a new Civil Charter could soon be adopted in the form of consideration of the work laid out by previous settlement commissions, even based on the 2009 text, which is a product of reconciliation. As the Nationalist Movement Party, we consider the need to prepare a new Civil Charter, which will allow for the discovery of a more democratic, fast, efficient, transparent, participating, and quality legislation, and we are ready to contribute and support in this direction. Finally, I'm going to go over a few issues with the House staff. Our councillors have problems, too. Although a common will has been established in the parliamentary law talks before to resolve the problems of our fellow assistants in the service class, there has still been no improvement. Those who work in the auxiliary service class must be taken into the general management service class. Our friends Kaves must be recruited, promises to stenographers, promises to be given the right to their share of weariness must be fulfilled. I wish our budgets to bring good results to our country and our nation, and I offer you my regards again.

tbmm-2018-12-11sit02spe0363par2062	The Public Control Agency is a constitutional institution in Article 74 of our Constitution. Under Article 6328 of Law IV, the Public Control Agency has been tasked with examining, investigating, and making recommendations to the administration on all actions and actions and actions of the administration over the functioning of the administration based on human rights, in accordance with the legal and rights of justice. Valuable MPs, the Public Control Agency, contribute to improving the quality of the administration's service, setting up good management principles, establishing the rule of law, spreading the rights culture; transparent, accountable, human-focused administration. We are the first political party to consider public control as the National Movement Party and to express the need for the establishment of the Public Control Agency. It's a very important area for this structure from our history and tradition to move forward in a healthy way, and we're following it. Dear lawmakers, the Public Control Agency is conducting its investigations and investigations on the basis of complaints. True and legal persons who are violated of rights and freedoms or interests as a result of the administration's conduct and attitude and behavior can be applied to the institution. Along with all individuals, NGOs, associations, foundations, companies have the right to apply and there is no prerequisite for applying. The Public Control Agency is trying to resolve disputes through reconciliation, recommending administrations to determine that the person's complaint is justified. In 2017 and 2018, the institution made several important recommendations, such as preventing rights violations in the delivery of children, the implementation of the family mediation establishment, the removal of rights violations in sports. A total of 57,739 complaints have been submitted to the Public Control Agency since 2013. 93 per cent of these complaints have been concluded. In 2017, again, 245 recommendations and 177 recommendations were partially rejected. Apart from the recommendations, 3,930 applications, including 2017 and 2018, have been resolved in friendly ways. Based on the last two years, the number of decisions the institution has made has been 1.172. Of course, there are shortcomings at the Public Control Agency. The institution has no authority to act on matters relating to the functioning of the administration. The fact that this authority has not been given to the institution is a significant flaw in its failure to provide the desired benefit from the institution. Advice decisions must be taken into account, and regulations on who will decide whether or not to abide by decisions must be made within the administration. According to institutional statistics, compliance with decisions is 65 per cent, which should be increased.

tbmm-2018-12-11sit04spe0571par0529	My dear friends, members of lawmakers, the amendment proposals to public administration budgets and amendments to the Constitutional Court of the 161st Article, which regulates budget and final account, are read and voted on without discussion. The presidency does not have the privilege of preparing budgets and implementing, according to other institutions. In the Constitution, there are no specific regulations for drafting, implementing, and auditing the Presidential budget. In a way that is incompatible with the principles of fiscal transparency and accountability, some practices that have excluded the presidential budget from the system have recently been carried out by provisions in the institution's budget (E) frameworks, which constitute a violation of the public financial management and control system. In Article 12 of Law 5018, which regulates the budgetary process, implementation and control of the institutions, the presidential budget is listed as part of the overall budget. In this regard, the presidency is part of the public administration under the central administration of Cetvel (1), added to Law 5018. Financial management and control of the institution's assigned resources are subject to Law No. 5018, the purchases of goods and services and the production of productions are subject to Law 4734. Therefore, he must use budget-substantiated funds within public spending law and account for them to the Turkish Grand National Assembly. In Article 4/1-c of the 2019 Central Management Law Proposal, titled "Cetves Tied Up" (E), the basis for the use and spending of some funds was shown on the ruler. As a matter of fact, the 2019 Central Management Budget Law, which includes the use of some funds and the basis for expenditures, consists of 79 articles. Even in recognition of the existence of an obligation to implement the presidential budget, these regulations must be included in the Court of Central Management Law (E). The rule of law in Article 2 of the Constitution is a state that is in accordance with the law, based on human rights, upholds and strengthens these rights and freedoms, maintains a fair legal order in all areas, and maintains it by developing it, prevents it from being in violation of the Constitution and from taking over all state bodies, which considers the law to be subject to the rule of law and rule of law and is open to judicial control. Laws are required to have general, objective, fair rules and to observe the laws of justice. While all public administrations meet their needs according to Law provisions 4734, the provisions of public tender Act 4734, added to Article 3 (y) titled "insults" (y), have already been removed from the scope of the presidential decree, as well as the promotion of offices and presidents, Turkey's promotion, investments in the country, or the purchases of goods and services of digital transformation and technological development. Some purchases of goods and services will be obtained according to the end of negotiations with the supplier, regardless of other public administrations, without any notices, without guarantees, without a tender commission, without a search for adequate rules for the willing. The maintenance, repair and all other operating costs of designated vehicles owned by the presidency's Executive Affairs Chairman are provided as part of a protocol with the THY Anonymous Partnership or its participation. The privilege of the Presidential budget in meeting some needs is incompatible with the measures that legal rules are fair and equitable. This motion is made because it is wrong to bring special regulations regarding the use of the funds assigned to the Court (E) in the section of the Central Management Act on the presidency of the 2019.
